# Islam Dunk: every line of game text

Generated by `npm run review:export` from the game source. Mark up `game-text.csv` in a spreadsheet (Keep / Change / Cut, suggested text, notes), or use the in-game review mode (`?review=1`). Row numbers match the CSV.

3403 lines, 2259 new since the Phase 1 packet (marked **NEW**).

| 645 | `career/office.js:60` | The Quran has 114 surahs, from Al-Fatihah to An-Nas. |
| 646 | `career/office.js:61` | Al-Fatihah is called Umm al-Kitab because it opens the Quran and gathers its core themes. |
| 647 | `career/office.js:62` | Al-Baqarah has 286 ayat, more than any other surah. |
| 648 | `career/office.js:63` | Al-Kawthar has only three short ayat, making it the shortest surah. |
| 649 | `career/office.js:64` | The Quran itself says it was sent down in the month of Ramadan (Al-Baqarah 2:185). |
| 650 | `career/office.js:65` | The first revelation came in the Cave of Hira, on Jabal an-Nur near Makkah. |
| 651 | `career/office.js:66` | The first words revealed were the opening of Surah Al-Alaq: “Read in the name of your Lord.” |
| 652 | `career/office.js:67` | At-Tawbah is the only surah that does not begin with Bismillah. |
| 653 | `career/office.js:68` | An-Naml opens with Bismillah and has it again inside the letter of Sulayman (27:30). |
| 654 | `career/office.js:69` | Ayat al-Kursi is verse 255 of Surah Al-Baqarah. |
| 655 | `career/office.js:70` | The Prophet ﷺ said Surah Al-Ikhlas equals a third of the Quran (Sahih al-Bukhari). |
| 656 | `career/office.js:71` | The Quran is divided into 30 ajza’, so many people read one juz’ a day in Ramadan. |
| 657 | `career/office.js:72` | The Hijrah in 622 CE marks the start of the Hijri calendar. |
| 658 | `career/office.js:73` | The Prophet ﷺ was born in Makkah. |
| 659 | `career/office.js:74` | He was born in the Year of the Elephant, when Abraha’s army marched on Makkah. |
| 660 | `career/office.js:75` | Badr, in 2 AH, was the first major battle between the Muslims and Quraysh. |
| 661 | `career/office.js:76` | The Battle of Badr took place in Ramadan, 2 AH. |
| 662 | `career/office.js:77` | At Al-Khandaq in 5 AH, the Muslims dug a trench to defend Madinah. |
| 663 | `career/office.js:78` | The Treaty of Hudaybiyyah was agreed in 6 AH and brought a truce with Quraysh. |
| 664 | `career/office.js:79` | Maryam is the only woman named in the Quran, and a surah carries her name. |
| 665 | `career/office.js:80` | Five daily prayers are obligatory: Fajr, Dhuhr, Asr, Maghrib and Isha. |
| 666 | `career/office.js:81` | Surah Al-Isra opens with the Night Journey from Masjid al-Haram to Masjid al-Aqsa. |
| 667 | `career/office.js:82` | Hadith encourage reciting Surah Al-Kahf on Friday. |
| 668 | `career/office.js:83` | Al-Kahf tells of young believers who sheltered in a cave and slept for many years. |
| 669 | `career/office.js:84` | Surah Al-Qadr says Laylatul Qadr is better than a thousand months (97:3). |
| 670 | `career/office.js:85` | Masjid Quba, built on arrival near Madinah during the Hijrah, was the first masjid the Prophet ﷺ built. |
| 671 | `career/office.js:86` | The qiblah was changed from Jerusalem to the Ka’bah in 2 AH (Al-Baqarah 2:144). |
| 672 | `career/office.js:87` | The revelation came over about 23 years. |
| 673 | `career/office.js:88` | The angel Jibril brought the revelation to the Prophet ﷺ. |
| 674 | `career/office.js:89` | Al-Fatihah has seven ayat and is recited in every rak’ah of salah. |
| 675 | `career/office.js:90` | An-Nas is the 114th and final surah in the order of the Mushaf. |
| 676 | `career/office.js:91` | Musa is named more than any other prophet in the Quran, over 130 times. |
| 677 | `career/office.js:92` | Surah Yusuf (12:3) calls its account “the best of stories.” |
| 678 | `career/office.js:93` | An-Nahl means “The Bee,” and the surah mentions honey as a healing for people (16:69). |
| 679 | `career/office.js:94` | The first revelation came when the Prophet ﷺ was about 40 years old. |
| 680 | `career/office.js:95` | Madinah was called Yathrib before the Hijrah. |
| 681 | `career/office.js:96` | Surah Al-Jumu’ah calls believers to hurry to the Friday prayer (62:9). |
| 682 | `career/office.js:97` | Surah Al-Fil recalls how Allah protected the Ka’bah from Abraha’s army with elephants. |
| 683 | `career/office.js:98` | Ar-Rahman repeats “Which of the favors of your Lord will you deny?” 31 times. |
| 684 | `career/office.js:99` | Al-Baqarah is named after the story of the cow in ayat 67 to 73. |
| 685 | `career/office.js:100` | The five pillars are the shahadah, salah, zakah, fasting Ramadan and Hajj (Sahih al-Bukhari and Muslim). |
| 686 | `career/office.js:101` | Belief in Allah, His angels, His books, His messengers, the Last Day and qadar, from the hadith of Jibril (Sahih Muslim). |
| 687 | `career/office.js:102` | The rites of Hajj take place in the early days of Dhul-Hijjah, the last month of the Hijri year. |
| 688 | `career/office.js:103` | Zakah is due on savings above the nisab once they have been held for a full lunar year (the hawl). |
| 689 | `career/office.js:104` | Zakah on money and trade goods is one-fortieth, or 2.5%. |
| 690 | `career/office.js:106` | Fajr is two rak'ahs. |
| 691 | `career/office.js:107` | Maghrib is the only obligatory prayer with three rak'ahs. |
| 692 | `career/office.js:109` | Fajr 2, Dhuhr 4, Asr 4, Maghrib 3 and Isha 4 add up to 17. |
| 693 | `career/office.js:110` | Maghrib begins when the sun has set. |
| 694 | `career/office.js:113` | The Prophet ﷺ chose Bilal, known for his strong, beautiful voice, to call the adhan in Madinah. |
| 695 | `career/office.js:114` | Wudu, the ablution, washes the hands, mouth, nose, face, arms, head (wiped) and feet (Al-Ma'idah 5:6). |
| 696 | `career/office.js:115` | When water can't be used, the Quran allows tayammum with clean earth (An-Nisa' 4:43, Al-Ma'idah 5:6). |
| 697 | `career/office.js:116` | The Friday prayer, with its khutbah, is prayed at Dhuhr time in congregation. |
| 698 | `career/office.js:117` | Tarawih are the extra night prayers many masjids pray in congregation during Ramadan. |
| 699 | `career/office.js:118` | The Prophet ﷺ said, “Eat suhur, for in suhur there is blessing” (Sahih al-Bukhari). |
| 700 | `career/office.js:120` | Eid al-Fitr is on the 1st of Shawwal, right after Ramadan. |
| 701 | `career/office.js:121` | Eid al-Adha is the 10th of Dhul-Hijjah, the day after 'Arafah. |
| 702 | `career/office.js:122` | Pilgrims stand at 'Arafah on the 9th of Dhul-Hijjah, the heart of Hajj. |
| 703 | `career/office.js:123` | Zakat al-Fitr is paid before the Eid prayer so everyone can celebrate Eid. |
| 704 | `career/office.js:124` | The Prophet ﷺ used to do i'tikaf in the last ten days of Ramadan (Sahih al-Bukhari). |
| 705 | `career/office.js:125` | The Prophet ﷺ said to seek it in the odd nights of the last ten of Ramadan (Sahih al-Bukhari). |
| 706 | `career/office.js:126` | Hijri months follow the moon, so Ramadan lasts 29 or 30 days. |
| 707 | `career/office.js:127` | The Hijri year begins with Muharram. |
| 708 | `career/office.js:128` | The Quran says the number of months with Allah is twelve (At-Tawbah 9:36). |
| 709 | `career/office.js:129` | Ramadan is the ninth month, between Sha'ban and Shawwal. |
| 710 | `career/office.js:130` | Shawwal follows Ramadan, and Eid al-Fitr is its first day. |
| 711 | `career/office.js:131` | At-Tawbah 9:36 mentions four sacred months: Dhul-Qa'dah, Dhul-Hijjah, Muharram and Rajab. |
| 712 | `career/office.js:133` | Abu Bakr led the Muslims after the Prophet ﷺ passed away in 11 AH. |
| 713 | `career/office.js:134` | Under Uthman, standardized copies of the Mushaf were made and sent out to the Muslim cities. |
| 714 | `career/office.js:135` | Abu Bakr hid with him in the cave, “the second of the two” (At-Tawbah 9:40). |
| 715 | `career/office.js:136` | Young Ali stayed behind in his bed, then returned the trusts people had left with the Prophet ﷺ. |
| 716 | `career/office.js:137` | Khadijah bint Khuwaylid was his first wife and the first to believe in his message. |
| 717 | `career/office.js:138` | Umar was called al-Faruq, the one who distinguishes truth from falsehood. |
| 718 | `career/office.js:139` | The Prophet ﷺ gave Khalid ibn al-Walid the title Sword of Allah. |
| 719 | `career/office.js:140` | Hamzah ibn Abd al-Muttalib, the Lion of Allah, was martyred at Uhud. |
| 720 | `career/office.js:141` | Abu Talib took him in at about age eight and protected him for years. |
| 721 | `career/office.js:142` | Abd al-Muttalib cared for him after his mother died. |
| 722 | `career/office.js:144` | Halimah of the Banu Sa'd nursed him in the desert. |
| 723 | `career/office.js:145` | Sumayyah, the mother of Ammar ibn Yasir, was killed in Makkah for refusing to give up her faith. |
| 724 | `career/office.js:146` | After the first Pledge of 'Aqabah, Mus'ab ibn Umayr went to teach the Quran in Yathrib. |
| 725 | `career/office.js:147` | Salman al-Farisi suggested the trench, a Persian defense the Arabs had not used before. |
| 726 | `career/office.js:148` | Abu Hurayrah narrated more hadith than any other companion. |
| 727 | `career/office.js:150` | The people of Makkah called him al-Amin. Amanah, trust, comes from the same root as our masjid's name. |
| 728 | `career/office.js:151` | The Battle of Uhud took place in 3 AH beside Mount Uhud. |
| 729 | `career/office.js:152` | Makkah was opened in Ramadan of 8 AH, with a general amnesty. |

| 731 | `career/office.js:154` | The expedition to Tabuk was in 9 AH. |
| 732 | `career/office.js:155` | The Farewell Hajj was in 10 AH, a few months before he passed away. |
| 733 | `career/office.js:156` | About 13 years in Makkah, then about 10 in Madinah. |
| 734 | `career/office.js:157` | He lived in Madinah for about ten years, from 1 AH to 11 AH. |
| 735 | `career/office.js:158` | He passed away in Madinah in 11 AH at the age of 63. |

| 738 | `career/office.js:161` | An-Najashi (the Negus) gave the Muslim refugees his protection. |
| 739 | `career/office.js:162` | Ja'far explained Islam and recited from Surah Maryam, and the king refused to hand them over. |
| 740 | `career/office.js:163` | In Ta'if he was rejected and stoned, yet he made du'a for their descendants to believe. |
| 741 | `career/office.js:164` | People of Yathrib pledged at 'Aqabah, which opened the way to the Hijrah. |

| 743 | `career/office.js:166` | Al-Muhajirun means the emigrants. |

| 1005 | `career/career-story.js:24` | **NEW** Ihsan is excellence for Allah, not for the crowd. |

| 1008 | `career/career-story.js:25` | **NEW** Do your best and leave the rest to Him. |

| 1035 | `career/career-story.js:37` | **NEW** That’s it? Just smiling? |
| 1036 | `career/career-story.js:37` | **NEW** Small deeds, done often, add up. Sadaqah doesn’t have to cost money. |
| 1037 | `career/career-story.js:37` | **NEW** You have a few hundred chances a game. |
| 1038 | `career/career-story.js:38` | **NEW** Lesson seven. Allah says: “Seek help through patience and prayer.” (Al-Baqarah 2:45) |
| 1039 | `career/career-story.js:38` | **NEW** Patience and prayer. He put them together. |
| 1040 | `career/career-story.js:39` | **NEW** Why patience and prayer together? |
| 1041 | `career/career-story.js:39` | **NEW** Patience holds you steady, prayer reconnects you. One without the other runs out. |
| 1042 | `career/career-story.js:39` | **NEW** That’s why we stop for salah even in the middle of a good practice. |
| 1043 | `career/career-story.js:40` | **NEW** What is patience during a game? |
| 1044 | `career/career-story.js:40` | **NEW** Running your offense when you’re down ten, not taking a crazy shot. |
| 1045 | `career/career-story.js:40` | **NEW** Sabr isn’t waiting around. It’s doing the right thing again and again without complaining. |
| 1046 | `career/career-story.js:41` | **NEW** Our last lesson for now. Allah says: “Indeed, Allah loves those who act justly.” (Al-Hujurat 49:9) |
| 1047 | `career/career-story.js:41` | **NEW** You’ve kept eight intentions. I’m proud of you. |
| 1048 | `career/career-story.js:42` | **NEW** How do I act justly in a pickup game? |
| 1049 | `career/career-story.js:42` | **NEW** Call your own fouls. Admit when the ball went off you. Give the other team the benefit of the doubt. |
| 1050 | `career/career-story.js:42` | **NEW** Pickup basketball is a test of honesty with no referee. |
| 1051 | `career/career-story.js:43` | **NEW** What comes after the lessons? |
| 1052 | `career/career-story.js:43` | **NEW** Now you teach them. One day a young brother will walk into this gym, nervous. |
| 1053 | `career/career-story.js:43` | **NEW** Be for him what we tried to be for you. |
| 1054 | `career/career-story.js:54` | **NEW** Before you walk out there. Every game, I ask the brothers one thing: what is your intention today? |
| 1055 | `career/career-story.js:54` | **NEW** Bismillah. What is your intention today? |
| 1056 | `career/career-story.js:54` | **NEW** Before we go out: what’s the intention? |
| 1057 | `career/career-story.js:54` | **NEW** Same question as always. What do you intend today? |
| 1058 | `career/career-story.js:55` | **NEW** Just let me play today. |
| 1059 | `career/career-story.js:55` | **NEW** Alright. Play well, and play with adab. |
| 1060 | `career/career-story.js:55` | **NEW** May Allah make it easy. Go. |
| 1061 | `career/career-story.js:55` | **NEW** Good. Hold onto that. Bismillah. |
| 1062 | `career/career-story.js:55` | **NEW** In sha Allah. I’ll be watching for it. |
| 1063 | `career/career-story.js:58` | **NEW** Before the game |
| 1064 | `career/career-story.js:74` | **NEW** You meant to |
| 1065 | `career/career-story.js:74` | **NEW** today. It didn’t quite happen, and that’s alright. |
| 1066 | `career/career-story.js:74` | **NEW** The Prophet ﷺ taught that whoever intends a good deed and doesn’t do it, Allah still writes it as a good deed. Renew it next game. |
| 1067 | `career/career-story.js:78` | **NEW** ✓ Intention kept: |
| 1068 | `career/career-story.js:78` | **NEW** HB for the masjid |
| 1069 | `career/career-story.js:79` | **NEW** You said you would |
| 1070 | `career/career-story.js:80` | **NEW** Come by my office when you have a minute. There’s something I want to teach you. |
| 1071 | `career/career-story.js:80` | **NEW** Come see me in the office. I have the next lesson ready. |
| 1072 | `career/career-story.js:84` | **NEW** You know, I played point guard at university. Before the beard went grey. |
| 1073 | `career/career-story.js:84` | **NEW** Wait, really? |
| 1074 | `career/career-story.js:84` | **NEW** Really. I passed, I prayed, I passed again. Not much has changed. |
| 1075 | `career/career-story.js:85` | **NEW** I can read where you’re going now, before you cut. That is trust. |
| 1076 | `career/career-story.js:85` | **NEW** On the court and off it, brothers who trust each other carry each other. |
| 1077 | `career/career-story.js:86` | **NEW** Full chemistry, masha’Allah. I think Allah put us on the same team for a reason. |
| 1078 | `career/career-story.js:86` | **NEW** To win? |
| 1079 | `career/career-story.js:86` | **NEW** To grow. The wins are a gift on top. |
| 1080 | `career/career-story.js:87` | **NEW** You’re not as slow as I said. |
| 1081 | `career/career-story.js:87` | **NEW** Thanks? |
| 1082 | `career/career-story.js:87` | **NEW** That’s a compliment. Take it. I don’t give many. |
| 1083 | `career/career-story.js:88` | **NEW** I was hard on you in 1v1 because nobody pushed me when I was young. |
| 1084 | `career/career-story.js:88` | **NEW** My dad worked nights. This gym was my second home. Uncle Mahmoud kept the key under the mat. |
| 1085 | `career/career-story.js:89` | **NEW** When you go up, I know where the ball is coming out. Every time. |
| 1086 | `career/career-story.js:89` | **NEW** You’re my brother now. Don’t make it weird. |

| 1088 | `career/career-story.js:90` | **NEW** Eleven? |
| 1089 | `career/career-story.js:90` | **NEW** Twelve if we count the du’a. |
| 1090 | `career/career-story.js:91` | **NEW** Real talk. I almost stopped coming to the masjid last year. |
| 1091 | `career/career-story.js:91` | **NEW** Then this team started. Now I come for Isha too. Don’t tell anyone. Actually, tell everyone. |
| 1092 | `career/career-story.js:92` | **NEW** Full chemistry! The handshake is finished. |
| 1093 | `career/career-story.js:92` | **NEW** Twelve steps, bro. The last one is helping the other team up. |
| 1094 | `career/career-story.js:93` | **NEW** Your numbers with me on the floor are up eleven percent. |
| 1095 | `career/career-story.js:93` | **NEW** Is that good? |
| 1096 | `career/career-story.js:93` | **NEW** It is statistically delightful. |
| 1097 | `career/career-story.js:94` | **NEW** I started keeping stats because I wasn’t sure I was good enough to play. |
| 1098 | `career/career-story.js:94` | **NEW** You passed to me anyway. That changed my spreadsheet. And me. |
| 1099 | `career/career-story.js:95` | **NEW** Full chemistry. I made a chart. |
| 1100 | `career/career-story.js:95` | **NEW** It’s just a heart. Please don’t show the others. |
| 1101 | `career/career-story.js:108` | **NEW** Lucky. Do that three more times and I’ll believe it. |
| 1102 | `career/career-story.js:108` | **NEW** Like I said. Al-Amanah is a nice youth group. |
| 1103 | `career/career-story.js:109` | **NEW** Let it go. It’s not worth it. |
| 1104 | `career/career-story.js:109` | **NEW** That’s wise too. They’ll be there next time if you change your mind. |
| 1105 | `career/career-story.js:110` | **NEW** I’ll answer him on the court. |

| 1362 | `career/career.js:312` | Assalamu alaikum! You must be the brother who just moved to Eastside. Welcome to Masjid Al-Amanah. |
| 1363 | `career/career.js:313` | Welcome to the gym! It's also the overflow musalla, the Sunday school, and the potluck hall. Watch out for folding chairs. |
| 1364 | `career/career.js:314` | The rim on the left is bent. Shoot at the right one. |
| 1365 | `career/career.js:315` | Beta, in 1987 I scored forty points in this gym. Nobody was keeping score, but I know. |
| 1366 | `career/career.js:316` | I'm Tariq, from the youth group. Somebody keeps score now. Every shot, every release, every assist. It's all on the sheet. |
| 1367 | `career/career.js:317` | And I'm Uncle Siddiq. Chai after Isha, and the chalkboard by the door keeps our streak. May it only go up. |
| 1368 | `career/career.js:318` | We're a small masjid, and we've never won the Metro Masjid League. But we play with adab, and we play for each other. |
| 1369 | `career/career.js:319` | You'll run the two-man game with me. Practice in the gym, earn Barakah Points, and get stronger. The league starts soon, in sha Allah. |
| 1370 | `career/career.js:320` | One shooting tip: let it go at the top of your jump. Watch the meter for the green. And don't force it with a hand in your face, pass instead. |
| 1371 | `career/career.js:323` | Al-Amanah is entering the league? Didn't your team lose to our youth group last year? |
| 1372 | `career/career.js:323` | That was a... strategic loss. |
| 1373 | `career/career.js:324` | Salaam, Jalal. May the better team win, and may we all pray Maghrib together after. |
| 1374 | `career/career.js:325` | Before every game I'll bring you a scouting report. Their record, their best player, one tip. Short. I promise. |
| 1375 | `career/career.js:326` | The State Masjid Championship. Eight of the best small masjids in the state. |
| 1376 | `career/career.js:326` | Travel light, pray on time, and don't eat gas station food before a game. |
| 1377 | `career/career.js:327` | Al-Burhan made it too. You beat us once. It will not happen twice. |
| 1378 | `career/career.js:328` | I packed two thermoses. One for us, one for Al-Burhan. Rivals still drink chai. |
| 1379 | `career/career.js:329` | Nationals. Brothers from masjids across the country, all in one gym. |
| 1380 | `career/career.js:329` | I looked up the other teams. They're good. |
| 1381 | `career/career.js:330` | We're good too! ...Right? |
| 1382 | `career/career.js:330` | We are. Our assist numbers went up every stage. I made a chart. |
| 1383 | `career/career.js:331` | We prepare, we make du'a, and we leave the rest to Allah. |
| 1384 | `career/career.js:332` | The World Ummah Cup. Istanbul, Kuala Lumpur, Lagos, Sarajevo. The whole ummah in one tournament. |
| 1385 | `career/career.js:333` | Whatever happens, you are representing all of us back home. Represent us well. |
| 1386 | `career/career.js:334` | Two teams from our country made it. Let us make sure one of us brings it home. |
| 1387 | `career/career.js:335` | Whatever the chalkboard says after this, I'm proud of all of you. Now go. The chai will wait. |
| 1388 | `career/career.js:338` | Good win. Now go help stack the chairs. Humility is also a muscle. |
| 1389 | `career/career.js:339` | Alhamdulillah. We thank Allah first, then we thank whoever grabbed those rebounds. |
| 1390 | `career/career.js:340` | Did you see that? DID YOU SEE THAT? |
| 1391 | `career/career.js:340` | Everyone saw it, Khalil. |
| 1392 | `career/career.js:341` | Very nice. In my day we also won. Stay hungry, but eat first. |
| 1393 | `career/career.js:344` | Sabr. A loss is a teacher if you let it be one. |
| 1394 | `career/career.js:344` | Free throws after Isha. I'll rebound for you. |
| 1395 | `career/career.js:345` | In 1987 we lost too. Then we had chai and came back. Have some chai. |
| 1396 | `career/career.js:345` | Next time we get them, bi idhnillah. |
| 1397 | `career/career.js:346` | Losing is not the shame. Losing your adab is the shame. You kept yours. |
| 1398 | `career/career.js:346` | We enter the tournament again. Same effort, more practice. |
| 1399 | `career/career.js:348` | Good game. You pushed us all the way. Do not quit now. |
| 1400 | `career/career.js:348` | He is right. Back to the gym. |
| 1401 | `career/career.js:350` | LOCAL CHAMPIONS! Put it on the whiteboard! Put it on every whiteboard! |
| 1402 | `career/career.js:350` | Alhamdulillah. First trophy in our history. Now, who is helping put the chairs back? |
| 1403 | `career/career.js:351` | Good game. You earned it. Go represent the city at State. |
| 1404 | `career/career.js:351` | The aunties are making biryani for the whole team. |
| 1405 | `career/career.js:352` | State champions, alhamdulillah. Remember how we felt after our first loss? Patience brought us here. |
| 1406 | `career/career.js:353` | We qualified for Nationals too. Save us a seat. |
| 1407 | `career/career.js:353` | Be humble in winning. The trophy is heavy, but a big ego is heavier. |
| 1408 | `career/career.js:354` | National champions. I don't know what to say. |
| 1409 | `career/career.js:354` | I do! ALHAMDULILLAH! |
| 1410 | `career/career.js:355` | We came from a gym with a bent rim. Now we meet masjids from all over the world. Let's be good guests and good ambassadors. |
| 1411 | `career/career.js:356` | World Ummah Cup champions. Subhanallah. |
| 1412 | `career/career.js:356` | Whatever we won, remember why we played: brotherhood, adab, and representing our community. |
| 1413 | `career/career.js:357` | In 1987 I scored forty points. But this... this is better. |
| 1414 | `career/career.js:357` | Can we fix the left rim now? |
| 1415 | `career/career.js:357` | We can fix the left rim now. |
| 1416 | `career/career.js:359` | Bend your knees on free throws. And your ego. |
| 1417 | `career/career.js:359` | Barakah is in sharing. Pass to the open brother. |
| 1418 | `career/career.js:359` | Box out. The rebound doesn't come to the one who waits. |
| 1419 | `career/career.js:360` | Hold shoot and let go at the top of your jump. Green on the meter is money. |
| 1420 | `career/career.js:360` | A contested shot is a gift to the other team. Find the open brother. |
| 1421 | `career/career.js:360` | Change direction while dribbling. The defender's ankles will need a break. |
| 1422 | `career/career.js:361` | Sprint at the rim and shoot: that is a dunk. Use it wisely. |
| 1423 | `career/career.js:361` | Pray before the game, not just after it. |
| 1424 | `career/career.js:361` | Defense is patience. Stay between your man and the rim. |
| 1425 | `career/career.js:362` | Help the man who falls, even if he wears the other jersey. |
| 1426 | `career/career.js:362` | Talk on defense. A quiet team gets screened. |
| 1427 | `career/career.js:362` | Stay low on defense. Tall legs, short temper, both are trouble. |
| 1428 | `career/career.js:363` | Shake hands before tip-off. Adab first, then basketball. |
| 1429 | `career/career.js:363` | **NEW** Drink water. Your legs will thank you in the last minute. |
